Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The math_logic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 796 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 263 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the math_logic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 796 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 263. QED.
